1	"""Pure temporal interval-matching metrics for rally evaluation.
2	
3	No I/O, no DB, no global state — every function here is deterministic and unit
4	tested. An "interval" is a `(start, end)` tuple of floats in seconds with
5	start < end. We score a list of *predicted* rally intervals against a list of
6	*ground-truth* intervals via temporal IoU and one-to-one greedy matching.
7	"""
8	
9	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
10	from typing import List, Tuple, Optional
11	
12	Interval = Tuple[float, float]
13	
14	
15	def interval_iou(a: Interval, b: Interval) -> float:
16	    """Temporal intersection-over-union of two intervals.
17	
18	    Returns 0.0 when they don't overlap (or either is degenerate). IoU is
19	    overlap / union, both measured as durations on the timeline.
20	    """
21	    a_start, a_end = a
22	    b_start, b_end = b
23	    if a_end <= a_start or b_end <= b_start:
24	        return 0.0
25	    inter = max(0.0, min(a_end, b_end) - max(a_start, b_start))
26	    if inter <= 0.0:
27	        return 0.0
28	    union = (a_end - a_start) + (b_end - b_start) - inter
29	    return inter / union if union > 0 else 0.0
30	
31	
32	@dataclass
33	class Match:
34	    """One matched predicted↔ground-truth pair."""
35	    pred_index: int
36	    gt_index: int
37	    iou: float
38	
39	
40	@dataclass
41	class MatchResult:
42	    """Outcome of matching predictions to ground truth at a given IoU threshold."""
43	    matches: List[Match] = field(default_factory=list)
44	    unmatched_pred_indices: List[int] = field(default_factory=list)  # false positives
45	    unmatched_gt_indices: List[int] = field(default_factory=list)    # false negatives (misses)
46	
47	
48	def match_intervals(preds: List[Interval], gts: List[Interval],
49	                    iou_threshold: float = 0.5) -> MatchResult:
50	    """Greedy one-to-one matching of predictions to ground truth by descending IoU.
51	
52	    Every candidate (pred, gt) pair with IoU >= threshold is considered; pairs
53	    are claimed highest-IoU first, and each prediction/GT can be used once. This
54	    keeps a single prediction from being credited for two ground-truth rallies
55	    (and vice versa).
56	    """
57	    candidates = []
58	    for pi, p in enumerate(preds):
59	        for gi, g in enumerate(gts):
60	            iou = interval_iou(p, g)
61	            if iou >= iou_threshold:
62	                candidates.append((iou, pi, gi))
63	    # Highest IoU first; ties broken by indices for determinism.
64	    candidates.sort(key=lambda c: (-c[0], c[1], c[2]))
65	
66	    used_pred, used_gt = set(), set()
67	    matches: List[Match] = []
68	    for iou, pi, gi in candidates:
69	        if pi in used_pred or gi in used_gt:
70	            continue
71	        used_pred.add(pi)
72	        used_gt.add(gi)
73	        matches.append(Match(pred_index=pi, gt_index=gi, iou=iou))
74	
75	    unmatched_pred = [i for i in range(len(preds)) if i not in used_pred]
76	    unmatched_gt = [i for i in range(len(gts)) if i not in used_gt]
77	    return MatchResult(matches=matches,
78	                       unmatched_pred_indices=unmatched_pred,
79	                       unmatched_gt_indices=unmatched_gt)
80	
81	
82	@dataclass
83	class Score:
84	    """Aggregate metrics for one (predictions vs ground-truth) evaluation."""
85	    iou_threshold: float
86	    num_pred: int
87	    num_gt: int
88	    true_positives: int
89	    false_positives: int
90	    false_negatives: int
91	    precision: float
92	    recall: float
93	    f1: float
94	    mean_iou: float            # over matched pairs (0.0 if none)
95	    mean_start_error: float    # mean |pred_start - gt_start| over matches, seconds
96	    mean_end_error: float      # mean |pred_end - gt_end| over matches, seconds
97	
98	    def as_dict(self) -> dict:
99	        return self.__dict__.copy()
100	
101	
102	def score(preds: List[Interval], gts: List[Interval],
103	          iou_threshold: float = 0.5,
104	          match_result: Optional[MatchResult] = None) -> Score:
105	    """Compute precision/recall/F1, mean matched IoU, and boundary errors.
106	
107	    Pass a precomputed `match_result` to avoid re-matching (e.g. when also
108	    extracting the failure lists); otherwise matching is done here.
109	    """
110	    mr = match_result if match_result is not None else match_intervals(preds, gts, iou_threshold)
111	
112	    tp = len(mr.matches)
113	    fp = len(mr.unmatched_pred_indices)
114	    fn = len(mr.unmatched_gt_indices)
115	
116	    precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) > 0 else 0.0
117	    recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) > 0 else 0.0
118	    f1 = (2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall)) if (precision + recall) > 0 else 0.0
119	
120	    if mr.matches:
121	        mean_iou = sum(m.iou for m in mr.matches) / tp
122	        mean_start_err = sum(abs(preds[m.pred_index][0] - gts[m.gt_index][0]) for m in mr.matches) / tp
123	        mean_end_err = sum(abs(preds[m.pred_index][1] - gts[m.gt_index][1]) for m in mr.matches) / tp
124	    else:
125	        mean_iou = mean_start_err = mean_end_err = 0.0
126	
127	    return Score(
128	        iou_threshold=iou_threshold,
129	        num_pred=len(preds), num_gt=len(gts),
130	        true_positives=tp, false_positives=fp, false_negatives=fn,
131	        precision=precision, recall=recall, f1=f1,
132	        mean_iou=mean_iou, mean_start_error=mean_start_err, mean_end_error=mean_end_err,
133	    )
134	
135	
136	def pr_curve(preds: List[Interval], gts: List[Interval],
137	             thresholds: Optional[List[float]] = None) -> List[Score]:
138	    """Score across a sweep of IoU thresholds (default 0.1..0.9 step 0.1).
139	
140	    Lets you see strict-vs-lenient behaviour rather than a single operating point.
141	    """
142	    if thresholds is None:
143	        thresholds = [round(0.1 * i, 1) for i in range(1, 10)]
144	    return [score(preds, gts, iou_threshold=t) for t in thresholds]
145

### 88. assistant (2026-06-06T14:33:03.993Z)

"""Export tuned WASB rally segments for manual eyeball verification.

After calibration picks a tuned threshold config, this turns a cached WASB
trajectory into the actual rally [start,end] segments that config produces, so the
user can answer "are the rallies actually better?" — by scrubbing the video to each
segment, by diffing tuned-vs-golden quantitatively, or by cutting clips.

Reuses (no new windowing/metrics/cutting logic):
  - calibrate_wasb.make_predict_fn  -> trajectory -> segments for a given config
  - eval.metrics.match_intervals/score -> tuned-vs-golden IoU comparison
  - tools.rally_slicer.slice_rallies   -> optional clip cutting (the exported CSV is
    written in the golden/slicer schema, so it feeds straight back in)

Run (uses the tuned config from the first calibration by default):
    python -m backend.eval.export_wasb_segments --trajectory ~/clips/sample_long_traj.csv \
        --labels "Annotation Setup/golden_labels_badminton.csv" --fps 59.94 --frame-width 1920
    # add --video <mp4> --slice  to also cut one padded clip per tuned rally
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import os
import os.path as osp
import argparse
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict, Optional

from backend.eval.calibrate_wasb import make_predict_fn, load_golden_gts, BASELINE
from backend.eval.metrics import match_intervals, score

Interval = Tuple[float, float]
Config = Tuple[float, float, float]

# The tuned config from the first per-video calibration (velocity, merge_gap, min_dur);
# F1 0.588 (baseline) -> 0.746. Override on the CLI to export a different operating point.
TUNED: Config = (0.05, 1.0, 1.0)

SEG_FIELDS = ["rally_number", "start_time", "end_time", "duration", "start_mmss", "end_mmss"]
COMP_FIELDS = ["type", "ref", "start_time", "end_time", "mmss", "status",
               "iou", "pred_start", "pred_end", "start_err_s", "end_err_s"]


def seconds_to_mmss(t: float) -> str:
    """Human-scrubbable timestamp, e.g. 106.031 -> '1:46.031'."""
    t = max(0.0, float(t))
    m = int(t // 60)
    return f"{m}:{t - m * 60:06.3f}"


def segments_to_rows(segments: List[Interval]) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    """Number segments 1..N in time order; emit the golden/slicer CSV schema."""
    rows = []
    for i, (s, e) in enumerate(segments, start=1):
        rows.append({
            "rally_number": str(i),
            "start_time": f"{s:.3f}",
            "end_time": f"{e:.3f}",
            "duration": f"{e - s:.3f}",
            "start_mmss": seconds_to_mmss(s),
            "end_mmss": seconds_to_mmss(e),
        })
    return rows


def _ensure_parent(path: str) -> None:
    os.makedirs(osp.dirname(osp.abspath(path)) or ".", exist_ok=True)


def write_segments_csv(path: str, segments: List[Interval]) -> str:
    """Write segments in the golden schema (rally_number/start_time/end_time + mm:ss).

    The schema is what tools.rally_slicer.load_rally_labels consumes, so the file
    doubles as input to clip cutting.
    """
    _ensure_parent(path)
    with open(path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=SEG_FIELDS)
        w.writeheader()
        w.writerows(segments_to_rows(segments))
    return path


def build_comparison(preds: List[Interval], gts: List[Interval],
                     iou_threshold: float = 0.5) -> dict:
    """Golden-centric comparison: per golden rally, did a tuned segment match (IoU),
    plus the boundary errors and any extra (false-positive) tuned segments."""
    mr = match_intervals(preds, gts, iou_threshold)
    sc = score(preds, gts, iou_threshold, match_result=mr)
    by_gt = {m.gt_index: m for m in mr.matches}

    rows: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
    for gi, (gs, ge) in enumerate(gts, start=0):
        m = by_gt.get(gi)
        if m is not None:
            ps, pe = preds[m.pred_index]
            rows.append({
                "type": "golden", "ref": str(gi + 1),
                "start_time": f"{gs:.3f}", "end_time": f"{ge:.3f}",
                "mmss": f"{seconds_to_mmss(gs)}-{seconds_to_mmss(ge)}",
                "status": "matched", "iou": f"{m.iou:.3f}",
                "pred_start": f"{ps:.3f}", "pred_end": f"{pe:.3f}",
                "start_err_s": f"{ps - gs:+.3f}", "end_err_s": f"{pe - ge:+.3f}",
            })
        else:
            rows.append({
                "type": "golden", "ref": str(gi + 1),
                "start_time": f"{gs:.3f}", "end_time": f"{ge:.3f}",
                "mmss": f"{seconds_to_mmss(gs)}-{seconds_to_mmss(ge)}",
                "status": "MISS", "iou": "0.000",
                "pred_start": "", "pred_end": "", "start_err_s": "", "end_err_s": "",
            })
    for pi in mr.unmatched_pred_indices:                 # extra tuned segments = false positives
        ps, pe = preds[pi]
        rows.append({
            "type": "pred_extra", "ref": str(pi + 1),
            "start_time": f"{ps:.3f}", "end_time": f"{pe:.3f}",
            "mmss": f"{seconds_to_mmss(ps)}-{seconds_to_mmss(pe)}",
            "status": "false_positive", "iou": "",
            "pred_start": "", "pred_end": "", "start_err_s": "", "end_err_s": "",
        })
    return {"score": sc, "rows": rows}


def write_comparison_csv(path: str, comparison: dict) -> str:
    _ensure_parent(path)
    with open(path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=COMP_FIELDS)
        w.writeheader()
        w.writerows(comparison["rows"])
    return path


def _with_suffix(path: str, suffix: str) -> str:
    root, ext = osp.splitext(path)
    return root + suffix + ext


def run(trajectory_csv: str, fps: float, frame_width: float, cfg: Config = TUNED,
        out_csv: str = "output/wasb_tuned_segments.csv", labels_csv: Optional[str] = None,
        iou: float = 0.5, baseline: bool = False,
        comparison_out: Optional[str] = None) -> dict:
    predict_fn = make_predict_fn(trajectory_csv, fps, frame_width)
    tuned_segs = predict_fn(cfg)
    write_segments_csv(out_csv, tuned_segs)
    total = sum(e - s for s, e in tuned_segs)
    print(f"=== WASB tuned-segment export ===")
    print(f"tuned cfg {cfg}: {len(tuned_segs)} rally segments, {total:.1f}s of play -> {out_csv}")
    result: dict = {"tuned_cfg": cfg, "num_tuned": len(tuned_segs), "out_csv": out_csv}

    if baseline:
        base_segs = predict_fn(BASELINE)
        base_out = _with_suffix(out_csv, "_baseline")
        write_segments_csv(base_out, base_segs)
        print(f"baseline cfg {BASELINE}: {len(base_segs)} segments -> {base_out}")
        result["num_baseline"] = len(base_segs)
        result["baseline_csv"] = base_out

    if labels_csv:
        gts = load_golden_gts(labels_csv)
        if not gts:
            raise SystemExit(f"no usable golden rallies in {labels_csv}")
        comp = build_comparison(tuned_segs, gts, iou)
        sc = comp["score"]
        comp_out = comparison_out or _with_suffix(out_csv, "_vs_golden")
        write_comparison_csv(comp_out, comp)
        print(f"\n--- tuned vs golden ({len(gts)} rallies, IoU>={iou}) ---")
        print(f"matched (TP) {sc.true_positives} | misses (FN) {sc.false_negatives} | "
              f"extra (FP) {sc.false_positives}")
        print(f"precision {sc.precision:.3f} | recall {sc.recall:.3f} | f1 {sc.f1:.3f} | "
              f"meanIoU {sc.mean_iou:.3f}")
        print(f"mean boundary error: start {sc.mean_start_error:.2f}s | end {sc.mean_end_error:.2f}s")
        print(f"per-rally detail -> {comp_out}")
        result["comparison"] = sc.as_dict()
        result["comparison_csv"] = comp_out
    return result


def _maybe_slice(video: str, segments_csv: str, clips_dir: str) -> None:
    """Cut one padded clip per exported rally (reuses the rally slicer)."""
    from backend.tools.rally_slicer import slice_rallies
    written = slice_rallies(video, segments_csv, ["all"], clips_dir)
    print(f"\ncut {len(written)} clip(s) -> {clips_dir}")


def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Export tuned WASB rally segments for eyeballing.")
    ap.add_argument("--trajectory", required=True, help="WASB trajectory CSV (Frame,Visibility,X,Y)")
    ap.add_argument("--fps", type=float, default=59.94)
    ap.add_argument("--frame-width", type=float, default=1920.0)
    ap.add_argument("--velocity-thresh", type=float, default=TUNED[0])
    ap.add_argument("--merge-gap", type=float, default=TUNED[1])
    ap.add_argument("--min-duration", type=float, default=TUNED[2])
    ap.add_argument("--labels", default=None, help="golden labels CSV -> tuned-vs-golden comparison")
    ap.add_argument("--iou", type=float, default=0.5)
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="output/wasb_tuned_segments.csv")
    ap.add_argument("--comparison-out", default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--baseline", action="store_true", help="also export baseline-config segments")
    ap.add_argument("--video", default=None, help="source video (with --slice) to cut clips from")
    ap.add_argument("--slice", action="store_true", help="cut one padded clip per tuned rally")
    ap.add_argument("--clips-dir", default="output/wasb_tuned_clips")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    cfg = (args.velocity_thresh, args.merge_gap, args.min_duration)
    res = run(args.trajectory, args.fps, args.frame_width, cfg=cfg, out_csv=args.out,
              labels_csv=args.labels, iou=args.iou, baseline=args.baseline,
              comparison_out=args.comparison_out)
    if args.slice:
        if not args.video:
            raise SystemExit("--slice requires --video")
        _maybe_slice(args.video, res["out_csv"], args.clips_dir)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""Tuned-segment export for eyeball verification (pure parts; no WSL/GPU)."""
import csv

from backend.eval import export_wasb_segments as ex
from backend.tools.rally_slicer import load_rally_labels


def _write_traj(path):
    """Synthetic trajectory: two moving segments separated by an invisible gap."""
    rows = [("Frame", "Visibility", "X", "Y")]
    for i in range(0, 30):
        rows.append((i, 1, i * 5, 50))
    for i in range(30, 50):
        rows.append((i, 0, -1, -1))
    for i in range(50, 80):
        rows.append((i, 1, (i - 50) * 5, 50))
    with open(path, "w", newline="") as f:
        csv.writer(f).writerows(rows)
    return str(path)


def _write_golden(path):
    with open(path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        w = csv.writer(f)
        w.writerow(["rally_number", "start_time", "end_time"])
        w.writerow([1, 0.0, 3.0])
        w.writerow([2, 5.0, 8.0])
    return str(path)


def test_seconds_to_mmss():
    assert ex.seconds_to_mmss(106.031) == "1:46.031"
    assert ex.seconds_to_mmss(6.031) == "0:06.031"
    assert ex.seconds_to_mmss(-5) == "0:00.000"


def test_segments_to_rows_numbers_in_time_order():
    rows = ex.segments_to_rows([(1.0, 3.0), (5.5, 8.25)])
    assert [r["rally_number"] for r in rows] == ["1", "2"]
    assert rows[0]["start_time"] == "1.000" and rows[0]["end_time"] == "3.000"
    assert rows[1]["duration"] == "2.750"
    assert rows[1]["start_mmss"] == "0:05.500"


def test_exported_csv_feeds_straight_into_the_slicer(tmp_path):
    """The export schema must be exactly what rally_slicer consumes (the reuse claim)."""
    out = str(tmp_path / "segs.csv")
    ex.write_segments_csv(out, [(1.0, 3.0), (5.5, 8.25)])
    assert load_rally_labels(out) == {"1": (1.0, 3.0), "2": (5.5, 8.25)}


def test_build_comparison_classifies_match_miss_and_false_positive():
    preds = [(0.0, 3.0), (5.0, 8.0), (20.0, 22.0)]   # 3rd has no golden -> false positive
    gts = [(0.1, 3.1), (5.0, 8.0), (10.0, 13.0)]     # 3rd has no pred  -> miss
    comp = ex.build_comparison(preds, gts, iou_threshold=0.5)
    sc = comp["score"]
    assert (sc.true_positives, sc.false_negatives, sc.false_positives) == (2, 1, 1)

    golden = [r for r in comp["rows"] if r["type"] == "golden"]
    assert len(golden) == 3
    assert [r["status"] for r in golden].count("matched") == 2
    assert [r["status"] for r in golden].count("MISS") == 1
    extra = [r for r in comp["rows"] if r["type"] == "pred_extra"]
    assert len(extra) == 1 and extra[0]["status"] == "false_positive"
    assert extra[0]["start_time"] == "20.000"


def test_run_writes_segments_and_comparison(tmp_path):
    traj = _write_traj(tmp_path / "t.csv")
    gold = _write_golden(tmp_path / "g.csv")
    out = str(tmp_path / "out" / "segs.csv")
    res = ex.run(traj, fps=10.0, frame_width=100.0, out_csv=out,
                 labels_csv=gold, iou=0.5, baseline=True)

    assert res["num_tuned"] >= 1
    # tuned CSV exists and is slicer-loadable with the reported number of rallies
    assert len(load_rally_labels(out)) == res["num_tuned"]
    # baseline + comparison artifacts written
    assert load_rally_labels(res["baseline_csv"])
    assert res["comparison"]["num_gt"] == 2
    with open(res["comparison_csv"], encoding="utf-8") as f:
        comp_rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))
    assert any(r["type"] == "golden" for r in comp_rows)

### 116. assistant (2026-06-06T14:37:17.310Z)

All three tasks are done. Here's the wrap-up.

## ✅ What landed this session

**(4) Repo cleanup**
- Collector was stranded on the merged `feat/validate-delivery` branch → synced to `master`, merged branch deleted. Both repos genuinely clean now.
- **PR #11 closed (branch kept).** Mid-task I caught that my "orphaned, superseded by separate repo" framing was *wrong* — there is no separate khelacharya GitHub repo; that folder is a second clone of this same repo, and PR #11 was the deliberate way shot-intelligence work is kept off `master`. I surfaced that, you chose "close, keep branch," and I fixed the stale memory that misled me.

**(2) WASB-pass checkpointing → [PR #25](https://github.com/avidullu/badminton-highlight-indexer/pull/25) (merged, now on master)**
- The reboot-loses-everything bug is fixed. Detector outputs cache per-window to `<out>_wasbcache/det_raw.jsonl` (fsync'd every 1000 windows) + atomic manifest; resume skips ahead to the first un-cached window; the stateful tracker is decoupled and re-run over the full ordered cache. A lost trajectory CSV now recovers in seconds.
- WSL-validated: tracker-only re-run **byte-identical**; incremental resume matches the full run except 3/300 rows by ≤6×10⁻⁵ px (inherent cuDNN noise, not a bug). 11 new tests.

**(1) Export tuned segments → [PR #26](https://github.com/avidullu/badminton-highlight-indexer/pull/26) (open)**
- New `export_wasb_segments.py` reuses the windowing, metrics, and rally slicer. Ran on the **real** trajectory + golden labels. 5 new tests; suite 187 green.

## 👀 What to eyeball (the actual deliverable)

Artifacts in `output/` (gitignored): `wasb_tuned_segments.csv`, `_baseline.csv`, and the per-rally `wasb_tuned_segments_vs_golden.csv`.

**Tuned vs 25 golden rallies:** 22 matched, 3 "missed", 12 "extra" → precision 0.647 · recall 0.880 · **F1 0.746** (exactly reproduces the calibration). Honest read: recall is strong and boundaries are mostly <1s, but precision shows the tuned config **over-segments**. Several "errors" are artifacts, not hallucinations:

- **Fragmentation:** golden rally 3 (`0:44.5–0:51.1`) got split into two pieces (`44.8–46.9` + `48.4–50.8`), so it counts as 1 miss **+ 2 false positives from one real rally**. That's a `merge_gap` symptom.
- **Boundary too long:** golden 8 (`1:58.1`, a 1.5s rally) detected as `118.1–123.0` → 1 miss + 1 FP.
- **Short rally dropped:** golden 7 (`1:35.6–1:38.0`, 2.4s).
- **Boundary overruns to spot-check:** rally 1 starts 6.2s late, rally 21 ends 4.8s long, rally 23 ends 3.0s long.
- **~9 genuine extras to watch:** `0:03.4`, `0:36.9`, `0:53.9`, `1:00.5`, `1:19.5`, `1:47.2`, `2:24.5`, `3:08.2`, `5:39.1` — could be warm-up, between-point motion, or real unlabeled rallies.

If you want clips instead of scrubbing: `python -m backend.eval.export_wasb_segments --trajectory <traj> --video <mp4> --slice` cuts one padded clip per tuned rally.

## Open threads / your call
1. **Merge PR #26** when you're happy with it.
2. **Eyeball verdict** — after watching, the levers for better precision are: raise `merge_gap` (fixes the fragmentation), revisit `min_window_duration` (the dropped short rallies). The *real* precision verdict still needs a **2nd labelled video** (`leave_one_video_out`).

Memory/handoff updated throughout. Want me to take a pass at a `merge_gap` sweep to attack the fragmentation, or leave it until you've eyeballed?

1	"""WASB-based hybrid play segmenter.
2	
3	Identical three-phase shape to TrackNetHybridSegmenter, but Phase 2 uses **WASB**
4	(nttcom/WASB-SBDT, MIT) shuttle-trajectory detection via WSL2
5	(backend/detectors/wasb_runner.py) instead of TrackNetV4. WASB ships pretrained
6	badminton weights and runs today, so this is the live shuttle-motion substrate.
7	Phase 3 (AI semantic boundaries) and Phase 4 (DB) are shared with the other hybrids.
8	
9	For the per-video *calibration* goal we mainly use Phase 2 (trajectory -> candidate
10	windows); the windowing thresholds (velocity_thresh / merge_gap / min_window_duration)
11	are what get tuned against golden rally labels.
12	"""
13	import os
14	import time
15	import logging
16	import concurrent.futures
17	from typing import List, Dict, Any
18	
19	import cv2
20	
21	from backend.indexer.base import BasePlaySegmenter, segmenter_registry
22	from backend.utils.video import get_video_duration, extract_video_chunk
23	from backend.sports import sport_registry
24	from backend.providers import provider_registry
25	from backend.detectors.wasb_runner import WasbRunner, WasbConfig
26	
27	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
28	
29	
30	def _fmt_ts(seconds: float) -> str:
31	    seconds = max(0, int(seconds))
32	    h, rem = divmod(seconds, 3600)
33	    m, s = divmod(rem, 60)
34	    return f"{h:02d}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}"
35	
36	
37	class WasbHybridSegmenter(BasePlaySegmenter):
38	    @property
39	    def name(self) -> str:
40	        return "wasb_hybrid"
41	
42	    @property
43	    def description(self) -> str:
44	        return ("Two-stage pipeline: WASB shuttle-trajectory detection (WSL2, pretrained badminton "
45	                "weights) for precise candidate rallies + AI provider for semantic boundaries.")
46	
47	    @staticmethod
48	    def _probe_fps_width(video_path: str) -> tuple:
49	        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(video_path)
50	        fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS) if cap.isOpened() else 0.0
51	        width = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH) if cap.isOpened() else 0.0
52	        cap.release()
53	        return (fps if fps > 0 else 30.0, width if width > 0 else 1280.0)
54	
55	    def process_video(self, video_id: str, video_path: str, player_pool: List[str] = None,
56	                      max_frames: int = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
57	        sport_name = self.config.get("sport", "badminton")
58	        try:
59	            sport_profile = sport_registry.get(sport_name)
60	        except KeyError as e:
61	            logger.error(str(e))
62	            return []
63	
64	        idx_cfg = self.config.get("indexing", {})
65	        provider_name = self.config.get("ai_provider", idx_cfg.get("ai_provider", "gemini"))
66	        model_name = self.config.get("ai_model", idx_cfg.get("ai_model", "gemini-2.5-flash"))
67	
68	        api_key_env_var = f"{provider_name.upper()}_API_KEY"
69	        api_key = os.environ.get(api_key_env_var)
70	        if not api_key:
71	            logger.error(f"{api_key_env_var} not set; cannot run the {provider_name} handoff.")
72	            return []
73	        try:
74	            provider = provider_registry.get(provider_name, api_key=api_key, model_name=model_name)
75	        except KeyError as e:
76	            logger.error(str(e))
77	            return []
78	
79	        video_duration = get_video_duration(video_path)
80	        if video_duration <= 0:
81	            logger.error("Invalid video duration.")
82	            return []
83	        fps, frame_width = self._probe_fps_width(video_path)
84	
85	        wasb_cfg = WasbConfig.from_indexing_cfg(idx_cfg)
86	        runner = WasbRunner(wasb_cfg)
87	
88	        w = idx_cfg.get("wasb", {})
89	        velocity_thresh = w.get("velocity_threshold", 0.02)
90	        merge_gap = idx_cfg.get("dead_time_merge_gap", 2.0)
91	        min_window_duration = idx_cfg.get("min_action_window_duration", 2.0)
92	        handoff_padding = idx_cfg.get("ai_handoff_padding", 2.0)
93	        ai_max_workers = idx_cfg.get("ai_max_workers", 5)
94	        log_candidates = idx_cfg.get("log_yolo_candidates", True)
95	        store_candidates = idx_cfg.get("store_yolo_candidates", True)
96	
97	        detection_params = {
98	            "detector": "wasb_hybrid",
99	            "velocity_thresh": velocity_thresh,
100	            "merge_gap": merge_gap,
101	            "min_action_window_duration": min_window_duration,
102	            "weights_path": wasb_cfg.weights_path,
103	        }
104	
105	        ok, msg = runner.healthcheck()
106	        if not ok:
107	            logger.error("WASB WSL environment not ready: %s", msg)
108	            return []
109	        logger.info("WASB WSL env OK: %s", msg)
110	
111	        # --- Phase 2: shuttle trajectory -> candidate rally windows ---
112	        t_start = time.time()
113	        out_dir = os.path.join("output", "wasb")
114	        csv_path = runner.run_predict(video_path, out_dir)
115	        if not csv_path:
116	            logger.error("WASB produced no trajectory; aborting.")
117	            return []
118	
119	        points = runner.parse_trajectory_csv(csv_path)
120	        logger.info("Parsed %d trajectory points (%d visible).",
121	                    len(points), sum(1 for p in points if p.visible))
122	
123	        all_candidate_windows = runner.trajectory_to_action_windows(
124	            points, fps=fps, frame_width=frame_width, chunk_start=0.0,
125	            velocity_thresh=velocity_thresh, merge_gap=merge_gap,
126	            min_window_duration=min_window_duration, chunk_index=0,
127	        )
128	        logger.info("Phase 2 (WASB) completed in %.1fs. Candidate windows: %d",
129	                    time.time() - t_start, len(all_candidate_windows))
130	
131	        if log_candidates:
132	            for cw in all_candidate_windows:
133	                logger.info(f"[WASB rally] {_fmt_ts(cw['start'])}-{_fmt_ts(cw['end'])} "
134	                            f"({cw['end'] - cw['start']:.1f}s) | frames={cw['active_frame_count']} "
135	                            f"peak_v={cw['peak_velocity']:.3f} mean_v={cw['mean_velocity']:.3f}")
136	
137	        candidate_ids = [None] * len(all_candidate_windows)
138	        if store_candidates:
139	            for i, cw in enumerate(all_candidate_windows):
140	                stats = {
141	                    "peak_velocity": cw["peak_velocity"], "mean_velocity": cw["mean_velocity"],
142	                    "active_frame_count": cw["active_frame_count"], "track_id_count": cw["track_id_count"],
143	                }
144	                candidate_ids[i] = self.db.add_candidate_segment(
145	                    video_id, detector="wasb_hybrid", start_time=cw["start"], end_time=cw["end"],
146	                    chunk_index=cw["chunk_index"], confidence=min(1.0, cw["peak_velocity"]),
147	                    stats=stats, detection_params=detection_params,
148	                )
149	
150	        if not all_candidate_windows:
151	            return []
152	
153	        # --- Phase 3: AI provider handoff (shared pattern) ---
154	        handoff_dir = os.path.join("output", f"chunks_{provider_name}_handoff")
155	        os.makedirs(handoff_dir, exist_ok=True)
156	        logger.info("Phase 3: %s validation on %d candidate windows...",
157	                    provider_name, len(all_candidate_windows))
158	
159	        def process_candidate_window(wi: int, window: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[dict]:
160	            w_start = max(0, window["start"] - handoff_padding)
161	            w_end = min(video_duration, window["end"] + handoff_padding)
162	            w_dur = w_end - w_start
163	            w_path = os.path.join(handoff_dir, f"handoff_{video_id}_{wi}.mp4")
164	            if not extract_video_chunk(video_path, w_start, w_dur, w_path, reencode=True):
165	                return []
166	            prompt = sport_profile.get_prompt(chunk_duration=w_dur)
167	            segments, _ = provider.analyze_video(w_path, prompt, chunk_duration=w_dur,
168	                                                 label=f"Handoff {wi+1}")
169	            valid = []
170	            for seg in segments:
171	                try:
172	                    seg["start_time"] = float(seg["start_time"]) + w_start
173	                    seg["end_time"] = float(seg["end_time"]) + w_start
174	                    seg["_candidate_id"] = candidate_ids[wi]
175	                    valid.append(seg)
176	                except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError) as e:
177	                    logger.warning("Dropping segment with unparseable timestamps: %s — %s", seg, e)
178	            try:
179	                os.remove(w_path)
180	            except OSError:
181	                pass
182	            return valid
183	
184	        ai_segments: List[dict] = []
185	        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=ai_max_workers) as executor:
186	            futures = [executor.submit(process_candidate_window, wi, cw)
187	                       for wi, cw in enumerate(all_candidate_windows)]
188	            for future in futures:
189	                ai_segments.extend(future.result())
190	        try:
191	            os.rmdir(handoff_dir)
192	        except OSError:
193	            pass
194	        logger.info("Phase 3 completed. AI confirmed %d semantic play segments.", len(ai_segments))
195	
196	        # --- Phase 4: DB population (shared pattern) ---
197	        segments_data = []
198	        for segment in ai_segments:
199	            try:
200	                start = float(segment["start_time"])
201	                end = float(segment["end_time"])
202	                dur = end - start
203	                shot_count = segment.get("shuttle_exchange_count")
204	                try:
205	                    shot_count = int(shot_count)
206	                except (ValueError, TypeError):
207	                    shot_count = max(3, int(dur / 0.8))
208	                metadata = {"shot_count": shot_count,
209	                            "shot_count_confidence": segment.get("shot_count_confidence", "Unknown"),
210	                            "detector": f"wasb-hybrid-{model_name}"}
211	                segment_id = self.db.add_play_segment(video_id, start, end, None, None, metadata)
212	                if segment_id:
213	                    candidate_id = segment.get("_candidate_id")
214	                    if candidate_id is not None:
215	                        self.db.link_candidate_to_segment(candidate_id, segment_id)
216	                    segments_data.append({"id": segment_id, "start_time": start, "end_time": end,
217	                                          "duration": dur, "metadata": metadata})
218	            except Exception as e:
219	                logger.warning("Skipping invalid segment struct: %s", e)
220	
221	        return segments_data
222	
223	
224	segmenter_registry.register(WasbHybridSegmenter)
225

1	import os

5	import logging

11	logging.basicConfig(
12	    level=logging.INFO,
13	    format="%(asctime)s [%(name)s] %(levelname)s: %(message)s",
14	    datefmt="%H:%M:%S",
15	)

17	from backend.database import Database
18	from backend.validators.base import registry
19	from backend.indexer import segmenter_registry

21	
22	def get_file_md5(filepath: str) -> str:
23	    total_size = os.path.getsize(filepath)
24	    hasher = hashlib.md5()
25	    processed = 0
26	    with open(filepath, "rb") as f:
27	        # Read in 1MB chunks for faster IO on huge files
28	        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
29	            hasher.update(chunk)
30	            processed += len(chunk)
31	            if processed % (1024 * 1024 * 50) == 0 or processed == total_size:
32	                print(f"\r[MD5] Processed {processed/(1024**3):.2f} GB / {total_size/(1024**3):.2f} GB ({(processed/total_size)*100:.1f}%)", end="", flush=True)
33	    print()
34	    return hasher.hexdigest()
35	
36	def main():
37	    video_path = r"C:\Users\avidu\Videos\Testing\KushagraYashAviFirstVideo.mp4"
38	    output_dir = r"C:\Users\avidu\Videos\Testing"
39	    output_filename = "Final_2_OutputOfKushagraYashAviFirstVideo.mp4"
40	    
41	    print(f"Loading config...")

43	        with open("config.json", "r") as f:

45	    else:
46	        config = {}

48	    print(f"Verifying files exist...")
49	    if not os.path.exists(video_path):
50	        print(f"Error: Video file not found at {video_path}")
51	        return

53	    os.makedirs("output", exist_ok=True)

56	    
57	    # Calculate MD5 of the video to uniquely identify it
58	    import time

60	    t0 = time.time()
61	    video_id = get_file_md5(video_path)

63

66

75	            choice = input("Would you like to (1) Run stitching on the cached segments, or (2) Reprocess the video with Gemini from scratch? [1/2]: ").strip()
76	            if choice == '1':
77	                print("[CACHE] Using cached play segments. Skipping segmenter API call.", flush=True)
78	                cache_hit = True
79	            else:
80	                print("[CACHE] Ignoring cache...", flush=True)
81	        else:
82	            print(f"[CACHE] Video exists in DB but contains 0 play segments. Treating as cache miss.", flush=True)
83	            
84	    if not cache_hit:
85	        print("[CACHE] Initializing clean indexing...", flush=True)
86	        db.clear_video_data(video_id)
87	        
88	        print("Bypassing OpenCV validations for performance on massive files...", flush=True)
89	        db.add_video(video_id, video_path, "processed", [])
90	        
91	        print("Running segmenter & indexing...", flush=True)
92	        seg_name = config.get("indexing", {}).get("default_segmenter", "yolo_hybrid")
93	        segmenter_cls = segmenter_registry.get(seg_name)
94	        if not segmenter_cls:
95	            print(f"[FAIL] Segmenter '{seg_name}' not found.")
96	            return
97	        print(f"Using segmenter: {seg_name}", flush=True)
98	        segmenter = segmenter_cls(db, config)
99	        segments = segmenter.process_video(video_id, video_path)
100	        print(f"Successfully indexed {len(segments)} play segments.", flush=True)
101	    
102	    if not segments:
103	        print("No play segments detected. Cannot compile highlights.")
104	        return
105	        
106	    
107	    end_padding = stitching_config.get("end_padding", 1.0)
108	    begin_padding = stitching_config.get("begin_padding", 0.5)
109	    min_shot_count = stitching_config.get("min_shot_count", 0)
110	    
111	    if cache_hit:
112	        print("\n--- Stitching Customizations ---")
113	        try:
114	            bpad_input = input(f"Enter begin_padding in seconds (default {begin_padding}): ").strip()
115	            if bpad_input:
116	                begin_padding = float(bpad_input)
117	                
118	            pad_input = input(f"Enter end_padding in seconds (default {end_padding}): ").strip()
119	            if pad_input:

123	            if shot_input:
124	                min_shot_count = int(shot_input)
125	        except ValueError:
126	            print("[WARNING] Invalid input detected, falling back to defaults from config.json.")
127	            
128	    print(f"\nCompiling highlights (with begin_padding={begin_padding}s, end_padding={end_padding}s, min_shot_count={min_shot_count})...", flush=True)
129	    
130	    # Filter segments where shot_count >= min_shot_count
131	    time_pairs = []
132	    skipped_count = 0
133	    for s in segments:
134	        meta = s.get("metadata", {})
135	        if isinstance(meta, str):
136	            try:
137	                meta = json.loads(meta)
138	            except Exception:
139	                meta = {}
140	        shot_count = meta.get("shot_count", 0)

143	        else:

148	    print(f"[FILTER] Retaining {len(time_pairs)} play segments for stitching.", flush=True)
149

152	        return

155	    try:

157	        print(f"Stitched highlight video saved to: {out_path}", flush=True)
158	    except Exception as e:

### 142. assistant (2026-06-06T14:47:31.756Z)

Read the real pipeline. One correction up front so we're aligned: I didn't check in any WASB weights this session — I checked in code (the resumable inference + the segment exporter). The actual weights are the pretrained wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar already sitting in WSL at ~/models/WASB-SBDT/pretrained_weights/. The demo will use those. Also worth knowing: the full wasb_hybrid segmenter's later phase hands each candidate rally to Gemini, which is the dead free-tier path we deliberately abandoned — so for this demo I'll run the shuttle-trajectory → windowing path directly (no AI, no stitcher), which is exactly the "segments as CSV" you're describing.

Here is the plan, plain text:

Inputs I need from you: the absolute path to the video file. That's it.

Step 0 — probe the video. I run ffprobe to read fps, frame width, duration, and frame count. These matter: the windowing normalizes shuttle velocity by frame width, and the segment times come from fps. I will NOT assume 60fps/1920 (those were the previous video's numbers). I'll report the probe back to you and a rough time estimate before the heavy step.

Step 1 — extract frames (fast path). I extract frames to JPGs in a temp folder using Windows ffmpeg (the proven fast route; the in-tool cv2 extractor is ~10x slower). Roughly 4GB and a few minutes for a ~6-minute clip; less for a shorter one. Deletable afterward.

Step 2 — WASB shuttle inference in WSL. I re-stage the current wasb_infer.py into the WSL WASB repo, activate the wasb conda env, and run inference over the frames with the pretrained badminton weights. Output is a trajectory CSV with columns Frame,Visibility,X,Y. This is the slow GPU step, but it now checkpoints — if anything interrupts it, re-running resumes instead of starting over. Expected throughput is about 27 windows/sec on this box, so I'll give you a concrete ETA after Step 0.

Step 3 — turn the trajectory into rally segments (no stitch, no AI). I run the exporter on the trajectory to produce the segment CSV: rally_number, start_time, end_time, duration, plus start/end as mm:ss so you can scrub directly. I'll generate two versions for comparison — one with the baseline thresholds (0.02 / 2.0 / 2.0) and one with the tuned thresholds (0.05 / 1.0 / 1.0) from the earlier calibration. Caveat I want to flag honestly: those tuned thresholds were fit on a different video (static-camera Sample Long Video), so on your new clip they're a starting point, not a guarantee — eyeballing both tells us whether they transfer.

Step 4 — hand off. I give you the CSV path(s) and a short summary (segment count, total seconds of play). No video gets stitched or written. You eyeball the source video against the timestamps and note observations.

What I will NOT do: no Gemini/AI handoff, no stitcher/compiler, no database writes, no committing the trajectory or frames (artifacts stay local/gitignored).

Two quick choices for you, or just say "go with defaults":
- Config: both baseline and tuned (my default), or only one.
- Frame coords: the CSV uses time only (seconds + mm:ss); say if you also want per-rally peak/mean shuttle velocity columns to help judge borderline segments.

Drop the video path and your go-ahead and I'll start with the probe.

---
name: feedback-tail-long-job-logs
description: User wants progress logs/summaries tailed to them during long-running background jobs, not just the final result
metadata:
  type: feedback
---

For any long-running background job (e.g. the WASB GPU inference pass, bulk fetches,
big test runs), the user wants **periodic progress surfaced to them while it runs** —
a tail of the log lines plus a short summary (% done, rate, ETA, and resource use like
GPU util/mem). This is a "dear ask": don't just report the final result.

**Why:** visibility into long jobs — the user wants to see it's alive and progressing,
catch a stall/regression early, and have an anchor on how long to wait.

**How to apply:**
- When you start a long background job, proactively tail it at sensible intervals
  (and at completion), not only when asked.
- A good snapshot = last few log lines + (windows/items done / total, rate, ETA) +
  GPU/CPU/mem where relevant. For WASB also show cached-windows / manifest windows_done
  (proves [[current-state]]'s checkpointing is keeping pace).
- For continuous tailing, offer `/loop <interval> tail the <job> logs`.
- Job logs live at the background task's output file (the path returned when the
  Bash/PowerShell job is launched with run_in_background).

### 187. assistant (2026-06-06T15:22:31.048Z)

Fun one. Let me Fermi it honestly — the per-ramp numbers I can defend; the fleet-wide number depends on Anthropic's user counts, which I don't have, so I'll keep that part as a plug-in-your-own formula rather than pretend I know it.

The unit being saved: one cold ramp-up = the DFS/BFS I'd otherwise do (a flurry of Glob/Grep/Read) replaced by a single ~5k-token map read.

Per cold ramp-up:
- Avoided input tokens (file contents + tool results dragged into context): ~50k–120k, call it ~80k.
- Avoided output tokens (the multi-turn "what do I open next" reasoning + tool-call args): ~5k–15k, call it ~8k. This is the *expensive* slice — output tokens cost ~4–5× input on price and are far heavier in FLOPs (autoregressive, one forward pass per token vs. a single batched prefill for input).
- Minus the map read (~5k in). Net ≈ 75k input + 8k output saved, and ~20–40 fewer tool round-trips (~30–90s less wall-clock).

Fleet math (parametric — these inputs are illustrative, not Anthropic data):
saved ≈ S·U·R, where S ≈ 85k tokens/ramp, U = weekly-active codebase users, R ≈ 8 cold ramps/user/week.
- U = 100k → ~68B tokens/week (~3.5T/yr)
- U = 1M → ~680B/week (~35T/yr)
- U = 10M → ~6.8T/week (~350T/yr)

CPU/GPU: tokens are the proxy for inference FLOPs. The ~8k saved *output* tokens per ramp is where the GPU-seconds live; at the 1M-user line that's ~64B output tokens/week not generated — real datacenter-hours, and it's the compounding kind because every avoided tool turn also avoids re-prefilling the growing context.

Network: ~80k tokens ≈ ~300–500 KB of text per ramp that never crosses the tool↔model↔client hops, plus the round-trip/latency tax of 25-ish calls. At 1M users that's ~3–4 TB/week — modest next to the token bill, but the latency win is what a human actually feels.

The honest caveats, because the headline number flatters itself:
- Prompt caching already kills most *intra-session* re-reads; the real prize is *cross-session cold starts* and post-compaction re-exploration.
- The map isn't free: generating a good one costs ~150k–500k tokens (this very workflow is in that range), and it drifts, so subtract regeneration/incremental-update cost. Break-even is ~2–6 reads — trivial for a repo you "frequently open," which is exactly your framing.
- Not every session ramps cold; warm continuations get nothing.
- It partly exists already — CLAUDE.md, `/init`, repo memory all chase this. The novel bit here is a *dense, anchor-rich, machine-first* map plus a *deterministic regen pipeline*, so it stays true instead of rotting into stale prose.

So: per-ramp, a clean ~10× reduction in ramp cost (85k → ~5k tokens, 25 tool calls → 1). Fleet-wide, "tens of trillions of tokens a year" is a defensible order of magnitude at 1M+ heavy users — dominated economically by the small output-token slice, not the big input one. Both background jobs still cooking; I'll surface them as they land.

1	# CODE_MAP.md
2	
3	**Purpose:** cold-start navigation map for the badminton-highlight-indexer — a self-hosted video → highlight-reel pipeline (CV/AI rally detection → SQLite → ffmpeg stitch) with a segmenter-agnostic eval/calibration stack.
4	
5	**Regenerate:** rerun the `build-code-map` workflow (parallel subsystem readers → this doc). Re-run after any registry/contract/WSL-interface change.
6	
7	**LEGEND**
8	- `@path` = file (always repo-relative)
9	- `::sym` = symbol (class/func/const) in the nearest preceding `@path`
10	- `->` = dataflow / call / "produces"
11	- `§` = data contract (canonical shape)
12	- `⚠` = gotcha / footgun
13	- `⎇` = registry / plugin extension point
14	- `WSL` = runs inside WSL2 conda env, NOT the Windows Python process
15	
16	---
17	
18	## 1. PIPELINE
19	
20	### 1A. Runtime: video file -> highlight reel
21	```
22	config.json (json.load; absent -> {})
23	  -> video_id = MD5(whole file)            @backend/main.py::get_file_md5  (⚠ chunk size varies per entrypoint: 64KB main, 1MB run_process, 8KB run_eval, 64KB phase3 — all hash WHOLE file so values agree)
24	  -> validate                              @backend/validators/base.py::registry.run_all(video_path, config)
25	       FormatValidator -> ctx['ffprobe_meta'] -> ResolutionFPSValidator -> PTSContinuity -> SceneCut -> Blur -> Relevance   (⚠ ACTUAL registration/exec order — see @backend/validators/__init__.py)
26	  -> db.add_video(video_id, filepath, status, [r.dict()])   @backend/database.py
27	  -> seg = segmenter_registry.get(name)(db, config)         ⎇ @backend/indexer/base.py::segmenter_registry
28	  -> segments = seg.process_video(video_id, video_path, player_pool?, max_frames?)
29	       [STOP POINT: segments-only — predictions now in DB; eval/regression operate here without stitching]
30	  -> select segment ids -> [(start,end)] (+ stitching padding)
31	  -> VideoCompiler.compile_highlights(filepath, time_pairs, out_name)  @backend/stitcher/compiler.py
32	       per-clip ffmpeg re-encode (libx264/superfast/crf22) -> concat -c copy -> output/<name>.mp4
33	```
34	Hybrid segmenter internal 4-phase (yolo_hybrid / tracknet_hybrid / wasb_hybrid — same shape):
35	```
36	P1 chunk video (yolo only; tracknet/wasb = whole-video single pass, chunk_index=0)
37	 -> P2 candidate "action windows" from motion/trajectory  [STOP: candidates persisted via db.add_candidate_segment]
38	 -> P3 pad clip (ai_handoff_padding) -> ThreadPoolExecutor -> provider.analyze_video(clip, prompt)   ⎇ provider_registry
39	 -> P4 db.add_play_segment + db.link_candidate_to_segment
40	```
41	Pure-AI path `gemini`: no P2; chunk-or-single -> provider -> heavy validate (clamp/dedup) -> add_play_segment.
42	Legacy `motion`: pixel-diff -> segments + **fabricated** metadata/events (⚠ not ground truth).
43	
44	### 1B. WASB shuttle substrate (P2 of wasb_hybrid)
45	```
46	WasbHybridSegmenter.process_video                @backend/indexer/wasb_hybrid.py
47	 -> WasbRunner.healthcheck()  (gate; not ok -> return [])   @backend/detectors/wasb_runner.py
48	 -> WasbRunner.run_predict(video, out_dir)
49	      stage video + wasb_infer.py into WSL fs -> `wsl bash -c 'source conda.sh && python wasb_infer.py ...'`
50	      -> @backend/detectors/wasb_infer.py::run (WSL): sliding windows -> GPU detector pass (CHECKPOINTED det_raw.jsonl) -> CPU tracker (always full re-run) -> trajectory CSV
51	 -> TrackNetRunner.parse_trajectory_csv(csv)      @backend/detectors/tracknet_runner.py  (shared, re-exported by WasbRunner)
52	 -> TrackNetRunner.trajectory_to_action_windows(points, fps, frame_width, chunk_start, velocity_thresh, merge_gap, min_window_duration, chunk_index)  [the model-agnostic WINDOWING BRAIN]
53	```
54	(`tracknet_hybrid` identical, swapping WasbRunner->TrackNetRunner; `_probe_fps_width` fallback (30.0, 1280.0).)
55	
56	### 1C. Calibration / eval flow (NO pipeline run unless told)
57	```
58	GT CSV -> annotations.load_annotations / calibrate_wasb.load_golden_gts -> List[Interval]
59	DB predictions -> harness.get_predictions(db, video_id, stage) -> List[Interval]   stage ∈ {candidates, final}
60	  -> metrics.match_intervals (greedy temporal-IoU) -> metrics.score -> Score
61	  -> harness.evaluate -> EvalReport -> report_to_dict
62	       -> batch.aggregate (corpus micro/macro)            @backend/eval/batch.py
63	       -> regression.regress(_with_provider) vs baseline   @backend/eval/regression.py  [CI gate]
64	Calibration: calibrate_wasb.run -> calibration.{select_best, improvement_report, cross_validate}  @backend/eval/calibration.py
65	Learned segmenter (offline): training.build_training_set(X,y) -> classifier.LogisticRegression.fit/save
66	```
67	Entrypoints: `@run_eval.py` (single video score), `@run_phase3_eval.py` (manifest batch, can segment), `@run_regression.py` (baseline gate, exit 1=regression), `@backend/eval/calibrate_wasb.py` + `@backend/eval/export_wasb_segments.py` (WASB tuning drivers).
68	
69	---
70	
71	## 2. SUBSYSTEMS
72	
73	### 2.0 Orchestration & config
74	- `@backend/main.py` — FastAPI app + argparse CLI (modes via FLAGS, not subcommands: `--server`/`--video-path`(process)/`--debug-clip`/`--trim-start`+`--trim-end`/`--setup`).
75	  - `::app` FastAPI; `::db_instance`,`::global_config` module globals set ONLY in `main()`. ⚠ importing `app` for ASGI without `main()` -> both `None` -> every endpoint NPEs.
76	  - `::process_video` `@POST /api/process` -> validate -> add_video -> segment. `::compile_highlights` `@POST /api/compile`. `::run_cli_diagnose_clip` lazy `import cv2`, samples ±3s vs `indexing.motion_threshold`.
77	  - ⚠ uvicorn host hardcoded `127.0.0.1`; CLI default_segmenter fallback `'motion'`.
78	- `@run_process_and_stitch.py` — one-shot demo; ⚠ hardcoded paths, interactive `input()` (blocks automation), `default_segmenter` fallback `'yolo_hybrid'`, skips validation.
79	- `@run_eval.py::main` — score one video's DB preds vs GT CSV. `--stage{candidates,final,both}`. Exit 2=arg/IO. ⚠ pure scorer; errors if video never processed.
80	- `@run_phase3_eval.py::main` — walk manifest, segment-if-needed, score, aggregate. `load_dotenv()` at import. ⚠ DB key = file MD5, NOT manifest's `video_id` (kept as label `collector_video_id`).
81	- `@run_regression.py::main` — `regress_with_provider` vs baseline. Exit codes semantic: **0=ok, 1=REGRESSION (CI gate), 2=missing DB**. ⚠ `--update-baseline` runs AFTER compare (snapshots even a regressing run).
82	- `@config.json` — single config. Sections: `validation` (+ `.relevance`), `indexing` (+ `.tracknet`; ⚠ NO `.wasb` block in checked-in config — WASB runs use `WasbConfig` defaults), `output`, `stitching`, root `sport/ai_provider/ai_model`. ⚠ **three default segmenters diverge**: config ships `gemini`, setup.py writes `yolo_hybrid`, main.py fallback `motion`.
83	- `@setup.py` — write default config (skips if present), ffmpeg/torch/CUDA diagnostics, GPU-aware pip (`nvidia-smi` -> cu124 else cpu). ⚠ generated config diverges from checked-in (no `relevance`/`tracknet` blocks, blur 100.0 vs 20.0).
84	
85	### 2.1 Segmenters (indexer) ⎇
86	- `@backend/indexer/base.py`
87	  - `::BasePlaySegmenter` ABC — `__init__(db, config)`; abstract `name`,`description`,`process_video`. § `process_video(video_id, video_path, player_pool=None, max_frames=None) -> List[dict{id,start_time,end_time,duration,metadata}]`.
88	  - `::SegmenterRegistry`, `::segmenter_registry` (singleton). ⎇ **register a new segmenter**: subclass `BasePlaySegmenter`, call `segmenter_registry.register(MyCls)` at module bottom, AND import the module in `@backend/indexer/__init__.py` (registration is an IMPORT side-effect). ⚠ `register()` builds `cls(None, {})` just to read `.name` -> name/description MUST NOT touch `self.db`/`self.config`.
89	- `@backend/indexer/__init__.py` — imports all 5 (`motion,gemini,yolo_hybrid,tracknet_hybrid,wasb_hybrid`) = the de-facto registration manifest.
90	- `@backend/indexer/wasb_hybrid.py::WasbHybridSegmenter` (name `'wasb_hybrid'`) — WSL WASB substrate. Config `indexing.wasb.velocity_threshold(0.02)` + shared `dead_time_merge_gap/min_action_window_duration/ai_handoff_padding/ai_max_workers`. ⚠ whole-video single pass; reuses YOLO-named keys `log_yolo_candidates/store_yolo_candidates`; hardcoded relative out dir `output/wasb`; needs `{PROVIDER}_API_KEY` (missing -> []). Registered at module bottom.
91	- `@backend/indexer/tracknet_hybrid.py::TrackNetHybridSegmenter` (name `'tracknet_hybrid'`) — copy-paste twin of wasb_hybrid (swaps runner; `detection_params` adds `queue_length`). ⚠ any P3/P4 fix must be applied to BOTH (and arguably yolo_hybrid).
92	- `@backend/indexer/yolo_hybrid.py::HybridYoloSegmenter` (name `'yolo_hybrid'`, no YOLO remains — torchvision detector + supervision ByteTrack). `::_PERSON_CLASS_ID=1` ⚠ (ultralytics used 0; wrong value -> zero detections). `::_lazy_load_model`, `::_extract_action_windows_from_chunk`, `::_DETECTOR_FACTORIES`/`::_DEFAULT_DETECTOR='fasterrcnn_resnet50_fpn'`. ⚠ velocity computed at `effective_fps = fps/frame_skip` -> `yolo_velocity_threshold` coupled to `frame_skip`; trackers per-chunk (windows merged by TIME not ID); `import torch` at module top.
93	- `@backend/indexer/gemini.py::GeminiPlaySegmenter` (name `'gemini'`) — pure-AI, no P2. ⚠ chunks only if `duration>chunk_duration`; `max_workers=5` hardcoded (ignores `ai_max_workers`); `max_segment_duration=90.0` hardcoded; `debug_duration` silently truncates analysis; `parse_time` accepts colon timestamps (warns).
94	- `@backend/indexer/motion.py::MotionPlaySegmenter` (name `'motion'`) — pixel absdiff baseline. ⚠ **metadata + serve/smash/foul/winner events are RANDOM** (random.sample/randint/uniform/choice); only segmenter using `db.add_event` and passing server/receiver; uses `print()`; honors `max_frames`.
95	
96	### 2.2 Detectors & windowing (WSL-quarantined)
97	- `@backend/detectors/__init__.py` — docstring only (refs `docs/DECISIONS.md` ADR-001). ⚠ does NOT re-export runners; import submodules directly.
98	- `@backend/detectors/tracknet_runner.py`
99	  - `::TrackNetConfig`(+`.from_indexing_cfg`), `::TrackNetRunner` — runs TrackNet `predict.py` in WSL.
100	  - `::to_wsl_mnt_path` (`C:\x`->`/mnt/c/x`), `::TrajectoryPoint`(frame,visible,x,y).
101	  - **`::parse_trajectory_csv` @staticmethod** + **`::trajectory_to_action_windows` @staticmethod** = MODEL-AGNOSTIC brain, re-exported verbatim by WasbRunner. ⚠ `weights_path` defaults `''` -> `run_predict` returns None; predict.py only `.mp4/.avi`; `visible==False` resets prev (occlusion gap breaks track); `fps<=0->30`, `frame_width<=0->1280` silent; `track_id_count` hardcoded 1; `timeout_sec=0` -> NO timeout (hangs forever).
102	- `@backend/detectors/wasb_runner.py::WasbRunner` (+`::WasbConfig`) — stages `wasb_infer.py` + video into WSL, runs in `wasb` env. `::parse_trajectory_csv`/`::trajectory_to_action_windows` = `staticmethod(TrackNetRunner.*)`. ⚠ `run_predict` does NOT pass cache flags (uses wasb_infer defaults flush=1000/batch=8/workers=4); `wasb_infer.py` MUST be re-staged each run (editing Windows copy is inert until staged); `_wsl_bash` duplicated (drift risk).
103	- `@backend/detectors/wasb_infer.py` (WSL) — `::run` core. Reboot-resumable detector cache: `::_DET_FILE='det_raw.jsonl'`,`::_MANIFEST_FILE='manifest.json'`, `::CACHE_VERSION=1`, `::load_and_clean_cache` (reads longest CONTIGUOUS prefix where `w==line index`, rewrites to heal crash-truncated tail), `::manifest_compatible`, `::_atomic_write_text/_atomic_write_trajectory`, `::_dets_to_tracker` (xy MUST be np.array). ⚠ GPU pass resumable; CPU tracker STATEFUL -> always fully re-run; `runner.gpus=[0]` hardcoded single-GPU; `extract_frames` cv2-PNG SLOW (prefer host ffmpeg).
104	
105	### 2.3 Eval & calibration (all pure; I/O lives in CLI drivers)
106	- `@backend/eval/metrics.py` — scoring primitive. `::interval_iou`, `::match_intervals` (greedy, deterministic), `::score`->`::Score`(`.as_dict()` = wire shape), `::pr_curve`, `::Match`, `::MatchResult`. ⚠ greedy not Hungarian; `score()` indexes the SAME preds/gts used to build `match_result` or boundary errors are silently wrong.
107	- `@backend/eval/annotations.py` — generic GT loader. `::load_annotations` (`start,end` CSV; RAISES on bad rows/`start>=end`), `::parse_timestamp` (decimal or MM:SS/HH:MM:SS), `::write_scaffold`.
108	- `@backend/eval/calibration.py` — overfit guard. `::cross_validate` (within-video k-fold; ⚠ defaults `metric='recall'` deliberately), `::leave_one_video_out` (honest cross-video; needs ≥2 labelled videos), `::improvement_report` (OPTIMISTIC — selected on full GT), `::select_best`, `::kfold_indices`, `::pct_improvement`, `::evaluate`. ⚠ `generalization_gap >~0.1` = overfit alarm.
109	- `@backend/eval/calibrate_wasb.py` — WASB tuning CLI. `::BASELINE=(0.02,2.0,2.0)` (⚠ hardcoded dup of real defaults), `::default_grid` (45 cfgs = 5×3×3), `::make_predict_fn`, `::load_golden_gts` (⚠ SILENTLY skips bad rows — opposite of annotations.load_annotations). ⚠ CLI defaults `fps=59.94/frame-width=1920` baked in.
110	- `@backend/eval/export_wasb_segments.py` — trajectory+cfg -> golden/slicer CSV + comparison. `::TUNED=(0.05,1.0,1.0)` (⚠ hardcoded from ONE video), `::SEG_FIELDS`,`::COMP_FIELDS`, `::build_comparison`. `--slice` requires `--video`.
111	- `@backend/eval/batch.py` — corpus aggregation. `::aggregate` (micro pool TP/FP/FN, TP-weighted mean_iou, macro F1), `::default_stages_for` (`auto`; `::_FINAL_ONLY_SEGMENTERS={motion,gemini}`), `::resolve_video_path`.
112	- `@backend/eval/harness.py` — DB-pred scorer. `::STAGES=('candidates','final')`, `::get_predictions` (candidates->`query_candidate_segments(detector=)`; final->`query_play_segments({})` ALL segs, detector arg ignored), `::evaluate`->`::EvalReport`(`::StageReport`)->`::report_to_dict` (input contract for batch.aggregate).

115	- `@backend/eval/regression.py` — CI gate. `::DEFAULT_TOLERANCE=0.05`, `::DEFAULT_BASELINE_PATH=output/regression_baseline.json`, `::regress`, `::regress_with_provider`, `::save_baseline`/`::load_baselines`, `::RegressionResult`. ⚠ imports `annotation_registry` from **`backend.annotations`** NOT `backend.eval.annotations` (two different "annotations"); gate fires on precision OR recall drop (F1 reported, not gated); first run on a video silently passes (no baseline).

133	### 2.6 Sports / Providers / Annotations / DB ⎇
134	- `@backend/sports/base.py::SportProfile` ABC (`name`,`get_prompt`,`get_relevance_config`); `@backend/sports/badminton.py::BadmintonSportProfile` (the Gemini prompt + HSV relevance cfg); `@backend/sports/__init__.py::sport_registry` (+`::SportRegistry`, auto-registers badminton). ⎇ **add a sport**: subclass `SportProfile`, `sport_registry.register(MyProfile)`. § prompt emits JSON array `{start_time(float DECIMAL SEC), end_time, shuttle_exchange_count(int), shot_count_confidence('Low'|'Medium'|'High')}`. ⚠ prompt actively defends against MM:SS timestamps; `get()` returns a fresh instance each call.
135	- `@backend/providers/base.py::AIVideoProvider` ABC (`__init__(api_key, model_name)`); `@backend/providers/gemini.py::GeminiProvider` (upload->poll->generate JSON temp 0->delete); `@backend/providers/__init__.py::provider_registry` (+`::ProviderRegistry`, auto-registers `'gemini'`). ⎇ **add a provider**: subclass, `provider_registry.register('name', MyCls)` (key passed EXPLICITLY, unlike sport_registry); `get(name, api_key, model_name)`. § `analyze_video(path, prompt, chunk_duration=0.0, label='') -> (segments:list, metrics:dict{upload_time,process_time,gen_time})`. ⚠ blocking `time.sleep(10)` poll; returns `([], metrics)` on ANY failure (empty != error); lazy client (auth surfaces on first use).
136	- `@backend/annotations/base.py::AnnotationProvider` ABC (`submit/poll/fetch/annotate`), `::Interval` (= metrics.Interval), `::STATUSES`, `::AnnotationProviderRegistry`, `::annotation_registry`. ⎇ **add a tier**: subclass (define `name`), call `annotation_registry.register(MyCls)` at module bottom + import in `__init__.py` (registry keys off `provider_cls().name`). § `fetch -> List[Interval]` sorted; contract says RAISE (not `[]`) on unavailable (`[]` silently zeroes recall).
137	- `@backend/annotations/file_provider.py::FileProvider` (`'file'`; job_id IS the resolved CSV path; -> `backend.eval.annotations.load_annotations`).
138	- `@backend/annotations/automated_provider.py::AutomatedProvider` (`'auto'`; injectable `labeler`, `::warn_if_same_lineage` ORACLE RULE, default `_not_configured` RAISES NotImplementedError). ⚠ get via `annotation_registry.get('auto', labeler=fn, lineage='...')`.
139	- `@backend/annotations/__init__.py` — ⚠ `noqa F401` imports are load-bearing (removing de-registers providers).
140	- `@backend/database.py::Database` — SQLite, 4 tables (`videos/play_segments/events/candidate_segments`), `PRAGMA user_version` migration ladder in `_init_db` (currently =1). Methods: `add_video`,`add_play_segment`,`add_event`,`add_candidate_segment`,`link_candidate_to_segment`,`query_candidate_segments`,`query_play_segments`,`get_video`,`clear_video_data`. ⚠ every conn re-issues `PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON` (else CASCADE silently skipped); `add_video` is `INSERT OR REPLACE` -> cascade-DELETES dependent segments on re-ingest; write helpers swallow exceptions (return False/None); events column is `type` not `event_type`; `duration` stored not derived.
141	
142	---
143	
144	## 3. DATA CONTRACTS (canonical shapes — one place)
145	
146	§ **Trajectory CSV** (produced by tracknet/wasb predict, consumed by `parse_trajectory_csv`):
147	`Frame,Visibility,X,Y` — header+column order fixed; `Visibility==1`=visible; X,Y pixel coords. Row = `(fid, 1|0, x or -1, y or -1)`.
148	-> `TrajectoryPoint{frame:int, visible:bool, x:float, y:float}` (frame-sorted).
149	
150	§ **Candidate window dict** (produced by `trajectory_to_action_windows` AND yolo `_extract_action_windows_from_chunk`):
151	`{start:float(sec), end:float(sec), active_frame_count:int, peak_velocity:float, mean_velocity:float, track_id_count:int(==1 for trajectory), chunk_index:Optional[int]}`.
152	`start/end = group_frame/fps + chunk_start`.
153	
154	§ **candidate_segments DB row** (`db.add_candidate_segment` / `query_candidate_segments`):
155	`{id, video_id, detector:str, chunk_index, start_time:REAL, end_time:REAL, duration:REAL, confidence:REAL(min(1.0,peak_velocity)), stats:JSON{peak_velocity,mean_velocity,active_frame_count,track_id_count}, detection_params:JSON{...}, confirmed_segment_id, human_label, notes, created_at}` (stats/detection_params deserialized on read).
156	`detection_params` keys by detector: wasb=`{detector,velocity_thresh,merge_gap,min_action_window_duration,weights_path}`; tracknet adds `queue_length`; yolo=`{velocity_thresh,merge_gap,frame_skip,tracker,detector_model,detector_score_threshold,min_action_window_duration}`.
157	
158	§ **play_segment dict** (`db.add_play_segment` / `query_play_segments`):
159	`{id:int, video_id, start_time:float, end_time:float, duration:float(end-start), server, receiver, metadata:dict, events:[...]}`. Hybrid P4 metadata `{shot_count, shot_count_confidence:'Unknown', detector:f'{family}-hybrid-{model}'}`; gemini detector = bare `model_name`; motion adds random `intensity/avg_speed_kmh`.
160	
161	§ **AI segment dict** (provider output, consumed by hybrid P3 / gemini): `{start_time, end_time, shuttle_exchange_count?, shot_count_confidence?}` (offset by chunk/window start; `_candidate_id` stamped internally).
162	
163	§ **GT / golden CSVs** (TWO conventions — do not conflate):
164	- generic (`annotations.load_annotations`): `start,end` 2-col; optional header; decimal or MM:SS/HH:MM:SS; RAISES on bad row.
165	- golden/slicer (`calibrate_wasb.load_golden_gts`, `rally_slicer.load_rally_labels`, `export.SEG_FIELDS`): `rally_number,start_time,end_time[,duration,start_mmss,end_mmss]`; SILENTLY skips bad rows.
166	
167	§ **det_raw.jsonl** (one window/line): `{"w":<window_index>, "f":[[frame_id,[[x,y,score,scale],...]], ...]}`. Lines MUST be contiguous (line N has `w==N`); first violation/JSONDecodeError -> truncate-heal.
168	§ **manifest.json** (cache): `{version, frames_dir(abspath), weights, sport, frames_in, frames_total, windows_total, windows_done}`. Reuse only if ALL match + `version==CACHE_VERSION`.
169	§ **collector manifest.json** (eval): `{eval_sets:[{video_id(label), local_path, csv}, ...]}`; ⚠ DB key = file MD5, manifest `video_id` is a label.
170	
171	§ **eval primitives**: `Interval=Tuple[float,float]` (start<end). `Match{pred_index,gt_index,iou}`. `MatchResult{matches, unmatched_pred_indices(=FP), unmatched_gt_indices(=FN)}`. `Score{iou_threshold,num_pred,num_gt,true_positives,false_positives,false_negatives,precision,recall,f1,mean_iou,mean_start_error,mean_end_error}.as_dict()`.
172	
173	§ **WSL detector candidate** (in-WSL, model<->tracker): `{'xy':np.array([x,y]), 'score':float, 'scale':int}`; plain JSON form `[x,y,score,scale]`. ⚠ `_dets_to_tracker` MUST rebuild `xy` as np.array (tracker does `np.linalg.norm`).
174	
175	---
176	
177	## 4. WASB WSL CONTRACT (external dep `~/models/WASB-SBDT`, MIT; NOT in this repo — claims below are unverified against external source, only the repo-side caller wasb_infer.py was checked)
178	- Env: WSL conda `wasb`, cwd `~/models/WASB-SBDT/src`. Hydra `compose(config_name='eval', overrides=[dataset=<sport>, model=wasb, detector.model_path=<weights>, runner.device=cuda, runner.gpus=[0]])`. `gpus=[0]` because box is single-GPU. (overrides VERIFIED in @backend/detectors/wasb_infer.py::_build_cfg.)
179	- `model=wasb` -> `name=hrnet`, `frames_in=3`, `frames_out=3`, input 512x288 (WxH), ImageNet normalize; affine resize maps heatmap coords -> original-frame coords. (model dims read from cfg["model"] at runtime in wasb_infer.run.)
180	- `detectors/detector.py::TracknetV2Detector.run_tensor(imgs, affine_mats) -> (results, hms_vis)`. `results[bid][eid]` = list of `{xy:np.array (ORIGINAL coords), score, scale}`. (caller uses `detector.run_tensor(imgs, trans)` returning `(batch_results, _)` — consistent.)
181	- `trackers/online.py::OnlineTracker` — STATEFUL, sequential per `update()` (assumes every frame fed once, in order, no gaps). `update(frame_dets)->{x,y,visi,score}`; `refresh()` resets. `det['xy']` MUST be np.array. Full ordered re-run is deterministic. (caller calls `build_tracker(cfg)`, `tracker.refresh()`, `tracker.update(...)` returning dict with `visi/x/y` — consistent.)
182	- `dataloaders/dataset_loader.py::ImageDataset(cfg, samples, input_wh, output_wh, transform, seq_transform, is_train)`; `samples=[{frames:[paths], annos:[{frame_path, center:Center(is_visible,x,y)}]}]`. (caller constructs exactly this — VERIFIED in wasb_infer.run.)
183	- Weights `pretrained_weights/wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar` — NOT committed, academic-data-trained -> ⚠ confirm terms before COMMERCIAL deploy. monotrack weights = noncommercial, avoided.
184	
185	---
186	
187	## 5. CROSS-REPO (sibling private repo `sports-data-collector` — external, unverified here)
188	- Indexer keys video by **FILE MD5**; collector keys by human `video_id`. ⚠ re-encoding/re-downloading changes bytes -> MD5; acquisition MUST precede processing; manifest tracks the exact file used.
189	- `python -m src.cli.curate export` (collector) -> `<out>/<sport>/<video_id>.csv` (indexer `start,end` format) + `manifest.json` -> consumed by `@backend/eval/batch.py` + `@run_phase3_eval.py`.
190	- Collector also emits golden rally labels (`rally_number,start_time,end_time,...`) -> consumed by `@backend/eval/calibrate_wasb.py` / `export_wasb_segments.py` / `@backend/tools/rally_slicer.py`.
191	
192	---
193	
194	## 6. GOTCHAS (cross-cutting footguns)
195	- **MD5 keying:** `video_id` = MD5 of WHOLE file everywhere — but each entrypoint uses a different chunk SIZE (64KB/1MB/8KB); values agree, code does not. Manifest `video_id` is a label, NOT the DB key.
196	- **Three default segmenters:** config.json `gemini` vs setup.py `yolo_hybrid` vs main.py `motion`. You may run a different detector than expected.
197	- **Gemini free-tier / API-key dead path:** hybrids + gemini return `[]` (not error) if `{PROVIDER}_API_KEY` missing OR WSL healthcheck fails — a silent no-op for callers. `analyze_video` also returns `[]` on real failure -> empty != "no rallies".
198	- **fps / frame_width must be PROBED not assumed:** `_probe_fps_width` / runner fallbacks (30.0, 1280.0) silently mis-scale velocity thresholds if probing is skipped; calibrate_wasb CLI bakes `59.94/1920`.
199	- **WSL `/mnt` can't see Google Drive / network mounts;** stage video into native WSL fs (`~/clips`) — `/mnt/c` reads are slow and Drive-backed paths invisible.
200	- **ffmpeg NOT in WSL** by design: all ffmpeg/ffprobe run Windows-side (must be on PATH); WSL only runs the GPU model. `extract_frames` uses cv2 PNG (slow) — prefer host ffmpeg + `--frames_dir`.
201	- **GPU float nondeterminism:** detector pass is checkpointed/resumable but not bit-reproducible; the CPU tracker re-run over the ordered cache IS deterministic.
202	- **`output/` is gitignored:** DB, baselines, cache, reels live there. Predictions MUST exist in `output/<db_name>` before any eval/regression.
203	- **Validator ordering coupling:** exec order is Format -> ResolutionFPS -> PTSContinuity -> SceneCut -> Blur -> Relevance; `resolution_fps_check` hard-fails unless `format_check` ran earlier (shared `context['ffprobe_meta']`).
204	- **`add_video` INSERT OR REPLACE + FK CASCADE** -> re-ingesting a `video_id` cascade-deletes its segments. Use `clear_video_data` deliberately.
205	- **Twin segmenters:** `wasb_hybrid.py` / `tracknet_hybrid.py` are copy-paste; fixes to P3/P4 must land in both.
206	- **Two "annotations" modules:** `backend.eval.annotations` (GT CSV loader) vs `backend.annotations` (provider registry). regression.py imports the latter.
207	- **Hardcoded tuned constants:** `calibrate_wasb.BASELINE`, `export.TUNED`, slicer/compiler padding — duplicated by copy, not import; re-tuning won't propagate.
208	- **`timeout_sec=0` = no timeout:** a hung WSL/GPU call blocks forever.
209	- **Stitch concat assumes identical encode:** per-clip re-encode then `-c copy` concat; if probe fails (0.0) no boundary validation.
210	
211	---
212	
213	## 7. RAMP PATHS (to do X -> open Y::symbol)
214	| Task | Open |
215	|---|---|
216	| Tune candidate windowing (velocity/merge/min-dur) | `@backend/detectors/tracknet_runner.py::trajectory_to_action_windows`; cfg `config.json indexing.{wasb,tracknet}.velocity_threshold`, `dead_time_merge_gap`, `min_action_window_duration` |
217	| Add a segmenter | subclass `@backend/indexer/base.py::BasePlaySegmenter`; `segmenter_registry.register(...)` at module bottom; import in `@backend/indexer/__init__.py` |
218	| Change stitching padding | `config.json stitching.{begin_padding,end_padding}`; impl `@backend/stitcher/compiler.py::VideoCompiler.compile_highlights`; mirror `@backend/tools/rally_slicer.py::BEGIN_PADDING/END_PADDING` |
219	| Add a sport | subclass `@backend/sports/base.py::SportProfile`; `sport_registry.register(...)` in `@backend/sports/__init__.py`; define prompt+HSV in new file |
220	| Add an AI provider | subclass `@backend/providers/base.py::AIVideoProvider`; `provider_registry.register('name', Cls)` |
221	| Add an annotation/GT tier | subclass `@backend/annotations/base.py::AnnotationProvider`; register + import in `@backend/annotations/__init__.py` |
222	| Run calibration (WASB thresholds) | `@backend/eval/calibrate_wasb.py::main` (`--trajectory --labels --fps --frame-width`); trust `cv` recall not `fit` |
223	| Export tuned rally segments / clips | `@backend/eval/export_wasb_segments.py::main` (`--slice` needs `--video`) |
224	| Run a video end-to-end | `@backend/main.py::main` (`--video-path` or `--server`) or `@run_process_and_stitch.py::main` (edit hardcoded paths) |
225	| Score DB preds vs GT (one video) | `@run_eval.py::main` |
226	| Batch score a collector manifest | `@run_phase3_eval.py::main` (`--manifest`) |
227	| Guard a regression (CI) | `@run_regression.py::main` (`--video-id`); exit 1=regress, 2=missing DB; snapshot via `--update-baseline` |
228	| Resume a crashed WASB pass | nothing to do — `@backend/detectors/wasb_infer.py::run` auto-resumes from `det_raw.jsonl`+`manifest.json` next to `--out`; `--fresh` forces restart; cache invalidates on weights/sport/frames change |
229	| Add a validator | subclass `@backend/validators/base.py::VideoValidator`; `registry.register(instance)` in `@backend/validators/__init__.py` (mind order; producers before consumers) |
230	| Add a DB column/table | `@backend/database.py::Database._init_db` migration ladder + bump `PRAGMA user_version` (else version-assert tests fail) |
231	| Train the learned segmenter | `@backend/eval/training.py::build_training_set` -> `@backend/eval/classifier.py::LogisticRegression.fit/save` |

## Code map — [PR #27](https://github.com/avidullu/badminton-highlight-indexer/pull/27)

`docs/CODE_MAP.md` is your cold-ramp artifact: ~260 dense lines, `@path::symbol` anchors throughout. Sections: pipeline (runtime + calibration/eval, with explicit STOP points), per-subsystem file/symbol index, canonical data contracts in one place, the WASB WSL boundary, the cross-repo collector contract, cross-cutting gotchas, a "to do X → open Y" ramp table, and a test→subsystem map.

The `map → synthesize → adversarial-verify` workflow is the part that makes it trustworthy — 8 agents, ~621k tokens, and the verify phase **caught and fixed real errors** the synthesis would otherwise have baked in (wrong validator execution order, a `config.json indexing.wasb` block that doesn't exist, a bogus `extract_yolo_features` gotcha) and honestly tagged the external WASB/collector sections as unverified-against-source. It's regenerable: rerun `build-code-map` after any registry/contract/WSL-interface change.

Wired into memory so future-me uses it: `code-map-artifact.md` (reference) + a "read `docs/CODE_MAP.md` FIRST for code" line in both `MEMORY.md` and the session-handoff ramp-up kit.

- **Open PR:** #27 (code map). master clean.

We need to identify a way to say "When both players are in "start stance i.e. one serving and one side receiving for singles or doubles" only then start a rally" or else it is overdetecting them. I know we are relying on a "shuttle trajectory" model so when the player send the shuttle by throwing it or hitting it by raquet, it probably shows up as a trajectory and gets detected. ### 219. assistant (2026-06-06T15:46:42.714Z)

Baseline's in, and the comparison is genuinely illuminating — it confirms your eyeball diagnosis hard.

**Baseline (0.02/2.0/2.0) vs Tuned (0.05/1.0/1.0) on Adarsh — `output/adarsh_baseline_segments.csv`:**

| | segments | play time | mean len |
|---|---|---|---|
| Baseline | 45 | 303.8 s | 6.8 s |
| Tuned | 65 | 251.4 s | 3.9 s |

Tuned is **+20 segments (+44%)** but **−52 s play (−17%)**, and the structural diff shows *why* that's bad, not good:
- **30 tuned-only segments**, durations almost all **1.0–2.0 s** (19 of 65 are ≤2 s) — these are your between-point service-feed false fires.
- **10 baseline-only segments** that tuned *lost* — and they're the **long rallies** (17.7 s, 10.9 s, 9.6 s, 9.2 s, 8.9 s, 7.6 s). Tuned's `merge_gap=1.0` **fragments real long rallies** so they no longer survive as single segments.

So on the honest read: **there's no "% improvement" here — if anything tuned regresses on this video.** No golden labels means I can't put a precision/F1 number on it, but the pattern is clear: the tuned thresholds were *overfit to the Sample Long Video* and don't transfer (this is exactly the `leave_one_video_out` caution made concrete). For Adarsh, **baseline is the better operating point** — fewer false fires, long rallies kept whole.

---

**The over-firing problem — your diagnosis is right, and there's a clean way to attack it.**

The data backs you: every false fire is a *short single trajectory* (the loser flicking/tossing the shuttle back for service registers as a 1–2 s "rally"). A real rally has the shuttle crossing the net **multiple times**; a service feed crosses **once**. That gives a spectrum of fixes, cheap → robust, all grounded in what we already have:

- **(A) Shuttle-only rally signature — no new model, highest ROI.** Gate each candidate on **net/midline crossings ≥ K** (≈ shot count) or trajectory direction-reversals, not just duration. A single toss = 1 crossing → rejected; a real exchange = ≥2–3 → kept. Implementable purely on the WASB trajectory we already produce. This alone should kill most of the 30 false fires.

- **(B) Your start-stance state machine — uses the person detector we already have.** WASB is shuttle-only, but the repo already ships a person detector (`yolo_hybrid`: torchvision Faster R-CNN + ByteTrack). At a legal serve both players are **stationary and diagonally positioned** in the service zones; between points they're **moving** (picking up the shuttle, repositioning). So: a state machine `IDLE → (await start-stance: players settled + diagonal) → ARMED → (shuttle trajectory) → RALLY → (rally end) → IDLE`. A trajectory that fires while *not ARMED* is rejected. Efficient version: **WASB proposes candidates cheaply; a stance-gate runs the person detector only in the ~2 s around each candidate start** to accept/reject — no full-video person pass needed (that path was ~50 min/video, which is why it was abandoned for proposal but is fine as a *verifier*).

- **(C) Pose-based serve detection — richest, new model + licensing review.** Detect the actual serve posture. Highest fidelity, most cost; probably overkill unless A+B fall short.

My recommendation: **A now** (fast, kills the documented pattern, shuttle-only), with **B as the robust target**. C only if needed. This needs a bit of research to do B right (how the badminton-CV literature — ShuttleSet, TrackNet-adjacent work — actually detects serves/rally boundaries), which is what you're sensing.

Let me capture this finding and then let you steer the next step.
